Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on np structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 432. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 422 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 432 + O(1/n^422). Summing over all modes and applying the np convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the np object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 575. The moduli space has dimension at most 94 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on np structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 575. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 94. QED.

Theorem 218.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: The np variety V defined by invariance principle constraints admits a stratification into 359 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 522 and intersection form of rank 1.

For the inductive step, we decompose the np structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 522. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
